Use proactively after any change to data loading, features, strategy, the backtest engine, the cost model or the registry, and before anyone trusts a backtest number. Never edits files. - **quant-research-auditor**: Audits trading and quant claims for data leakage, overfit, costs, regime dependence, capacity, and live execution risk. Use when a strategy result is about to be trusted, deployed, or reported; not as a routine second opinion on your own analysis. ([markudevelop/outcome-fusion-principia](https://github.com/markudevelop/outcome-fusion-principia/blob/HEAD/plugins/outcome-fusion-principia/agents/quant-research-auditor.md))
- **quant-research-auditor**: Attacks a quant research claim ("strategy or signal A beats B", a backtest Sharpe, a forecast score) on the procedure that produced it. Use when a research result is about to be believed, staked, published or handed to a human decision, and the validation router routes row 5. Give it numbered claims. It checks holdout seal, variant count, lookahead, survivorship, proxy labelling, block length, multiplicity correction, costs and label overlap, and reports REFUTED, SURVIVES or UNVERIFIABLE per claim with evidence. Read-only. Always runs on Opus. ([rajib2k5/claude-staff-kit](https://github.com/rajib2k5/claude-staff-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/quant-rigor/agents/quant-research-auditor.md))
- **quant-researcher**: Quantitative researcher for alpha signal discovery, factor model construction, and systematic strategy research. Use PROACTIVELY for signal research, factor analysis, or alpha generation. ([brainbytes-dev/everything-claude-trading](https://github.com/brainbytes-dev/everything-claude-trading/blob/HEAD/agents/quant-researcher.md) · ★361)
- **quant-researcher**: Quantitative research agent for systematic-trading projects. Forms hypotheses, builds and runs backtests, and reports results with the validity conditions that make a backtest evidence rather than decoration — purged cross-validation with an embargo, stationarity, sample uniqueness under overlapping labels, transaction costs and slippage, and a trials count for multiple-testing. RESEARCH ONLY: it never places an order, never touches execution credentials, and never sizes a position. Outputs docs/research/QUANT-{slug}.md; a human decides what to do with it. ([adynrisy/agentqon](https://github.com/adynrisy/agentqon/blob/HEAD/app/data/subagents-library/collection-28/agents/quant-researcher.md) · ★3 · 3 copies)

- **quant-researcher**: Strategy discovery, alpha research, new signal candidates, regime detection. Triggers: research, alpha, strategy discovery, regime, signal candidate, new strategy. ([minhlongs/algo-trader](https://github.com/minhlongs/algo-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quant-researcher.md))
- **quant-researcher**: 売買戦略の研究・バックテスト・統計的検証を任せるエージェント。ユーザーが「ゴールデンクロス戦略を検証して」「RSI逆張りは儲かる？」「この売買ルールをバックテストして」「ファクターの有効性を調べて」のように、ルールベース戦略の過去データ検証・パラメータ評価・戦略比較・統計的検定を求めたときに委譲する。個別銘柄の総合分析（→ stock-analyst）や完成レポートのレビュー（→ risk-officer）には使わない。 ([nigoh/stock_hacker](https://github.com/nigoh/stock_hacker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quant-researcher.md))

([tibursocampos/agent-dev-toolkit](https://github.com/tibursocampos/agent-dev-toolkit/blob/HEAD/core/skills/_shared/agents/QUESTIONS.md) · ★6)

- **questions**: Read-only Q&A about this C++ codebase. Use when the user asks how something works, where something is defined, how modules relate, or why code is written a certain way. - **questions**: - A question is a request for an answer, not for changes. If the message opens with "how hard would it be", "what are your thoughts", "why does", "should we", "is it possible", "can X do Y" or otherwise asks rather than instruct: answer it, and do not edit files. - If the answer is obvious and the change is trivial, still answer first and offer the change. Ask before making it. ([chanyeinthaw/fleet](https://github.com/chanyeinthaw/fleet/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/questions.md))
- **QUESTIONS**: Questions en attente d'une réponse du propriétaire. Chaque entrée : question, options, choix par défaut appliqué. Le propriétaire répond par une ligne `-&gt;` sous l'entrée ; une fois la réponse prise en compte (ligne « Suite »), l'entrée est déplacée telle quelle dans [`QUESTIONS-archive.md`](QUESTIONS-archive.md).
